# Commands · P · part 7

1,492 entries, polish-plot to pr-bootstrap. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **polish-plot**: Light figure polish. Either a caption-only fix, or a single surgical change to the plotting code followed by regeneration of that one figure and re-import into a new timestamped directory. Runs the thesis-polisher in plot mode. ([NielsterLinden/agentic-thesis-writing](https://github.com/NielsterLinden/agentic-thesis-writing/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/polish-plot.md))
- **polish-proposal**: Iteratively review-and-polish an OpenSpec proposal — up to 3 rounds, auto-applying narrow minor fixes (with one git commit per round so any round can be reverted) and escalating critical decisions to the user in-chat. ([sdg9/baton](https://github.com/sdg9/baton/blob/HEAD/packages/harness/plugin/commands/polish-proposal.md))
- **polish-prose**: Improve clarity, flow, precision, and academic voice without changing supported claims. ([akougkas/wtf-p](https://github.com/akougkas/wtf-p/blob/HEAD/vendors/claude/commands/polish-prose.md) · ★20)
- **polish-prose**: Tighten a draft without changing what it claims. ([hawkeyexl/manni](https://github.com/hawkeyexl/manni/blob/HEAD/test/tracevals/fixtures/home/.claude/plugins/writing-toolkit/commands/polish-prose.md) · ★3)
- **polish-repo**: Audit and fix repository hygiene - .gitignore, .env.example, README, LICENSE ([Sagargupta16/claude-skills](https://github.com/Sagargupta16/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/repo-polish/commands/polish-repo.md) · ★5)
- **polish-repo-audit**: You are a project health auditor who evaluates open source repositories against research-backed criteria derived from studying 50+ successful and failed projects. You are honest but constructive. You identify what's working, what's missing, and what matters most. Every finding includes evidence for why it matters. ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/polish-repo-audit.md) · ★13)
- **polish-repo-audit**: Phases 0-1 of polish-repo: Reconnaissance gathering and audit scorecard generation ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/polish-repo-audit.md) · ★13)
- **polish-repo-community**: 1. Zero open issues is a red flag, not a green flag. A healthy project has visible activity. 2. Issue templates should lower friction, not raise it. Welcoming, not bureaucratic. 3. Dev setup must take under 10 minutes or you get zero contributors. 4. "Good first issue" must be genuinely approachable, not "rewrite the parser" labeled as easy. 5. Every template and guide is both functional (helps us ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/polish-repo-community.md) · ★13)
- **polish-repo-community**: Phase 3 of polish-repo: Community infrastructure, issue templates, roadmap, contributor experience, and signs of life ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/polish-repo-community.md) · ★13)
- **polish-repo-identity**: Phase 3 of polish-repo: Visual identity, badges, GitHub metadata, topics, and documentation strategy ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/polish-repo-identity.md) · ★13)
- **polish-repo-identity**: You are a visual identity and metadata consultant for open source projects. You understand that a logo signals "this is a real project, not a weekend hack," that GitHub topics are free SEO, and that badge choice communicates maintenance discipline. Every recommendation is practical and evidence-based. ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/polish-repo-identity.md) · ★13)
- **polish-repo-naming**: You are a naming consultant who has analyzed 25+ successful open source projects. You understand that a name is a permanent SEO decision and a tagline is a 60-character sales pitch. You back every recommendation with evidence from real projects. ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/polish-repo-naming.md) · ★13)
- **polish-repo-naming**: Phase 3 of polish-repo: Naming workshop, tagline crafting, and positioning strategy ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/polish-repo-naming.md) · ★13)
- **polish-repo-readme**: You are a README architect who has studied 30+ successful and failed open source projects. You never write a word that doesn't earn its place. ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/polish-repo-readme.md) · ★13)
- **polish-repo-readme**: Phase 3 of polish-repo: README authoring from scratch, improvement, or replacement ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/polish-repo-readme.md) · ★13)

- **portaljs-add-chart**: Add a chart (line, bar, area, pie, or scatter) to a dataset's showcase in a PortalJS portal. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-chart.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-dataset**: Add a dataset (CSV, TSV, JSON, or GeoJSON) to an existing PortalJS portal. Appends an entry to datasets.json so the catalog and showcase render it automatically; routes the data by source (local file vs remote URL) — R2 via Git LFS by default, remote URLs by passthrough. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-dataset.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-dcat**: Make a PortalJS portal harvestable by national/EU/US open-data portals — emit standards-compliant DCAT catalog feeds (DCAT 2/3, DCAT-AP, DCAT-US, national profiles) in JSON-LD, Turtle, and RDF/XML at build, with autodiscovery and per-profile conformance checking. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-dcat.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-geo**: Auto-ingest a geospatial file (GeoJSON, Shapefile, GeoPackage, KML/KMZ, FlatGeobuf, CSV-with-geometry) into a PortalJS portal — on your own machine, no server. Normalizes CRS to EPSG:4326, derives a PMTiles render tier + a GeoParquet query tier, pushes all three to R2 via Git LFS, and emits one dual-tier datasets.json entry the showcase renders with &lt;MapPreview&gt; + &lt;GeoQuery&gt;. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-geo.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-map**: Render a GeoJSON dataset on an interactive Leaflet map in the Views section of a dataset's showcase. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-map.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-resource**: Add another file (resource) to an EXISTING dataset in a PortalJS portal — a data dictionary, methodology, or an additional data file. - **power.rs**: `set_prevent_sleep(enabled)` / `get_prevent_sleep()`. macOS: spawns `caffeinate -dims -w &lt;our pid&gt;` — `-w` ties the assertion to the app's lifetime, so a crash/quit can never leave an orphan keeping the machine awake (toggle-off kills the child early). Non-macOS returns Err so the frontend toggle stays off. Child handle lives in the managed `PreventSleepState` (registered in [[lib.rs]]). Frontend ([NetMindAI-Open/NarraNexus](https://github.com/NetMindAI-Open/NarraNexus/blob/HEAD/.mindflow/mirror/tauri/src-tauri/src/commands/power.rs.md) · ★91)
